### ea2ce5344bd2d76bb34c2844e142442527d06a7e

[ea2ce5344bd2d76bb34c2844e142442527d06a7e](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/ea2ce5344bd2d76bb34c2844e142442527d06a7e)

Date: 2026-08-08T01:28:46-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Networking & Sessions, UI, Build & Tooling, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add hub lobby session launcher and scene
```

#### Original body

```text
Introduces a new `Lobby-Town` scene and wires it into build settings for shared-mode hub entry. Adds hub networking flow components (`HubSessionLauncher`, runner factory, and player spawner) plus a temporary inspector debug starter to start/shutdown sessions manually. Also adds a play mode test covering session start/shutdown behavior and updates the minimap prefab test to check for cameras from the parent `RaidMinimapView` hierarchy.
```

#### Original subject

```text
Document town shared mode architecture
```

#### Original body

```text
Adds a new architecture spec for the Town/Pueblo as a persistent Fusion Shared Mode hub, explicitly separating it from Dungeon Host/Client sessions and defining launcher, runner, and spawner contracts. Updates AGENTS.md to reflect this networking split, and expands SessionLifecycleAndSpawning.md with a full lifecycle reference, stricter spawn/load policies, and clearer Host Migration restoration and edge-case behavior.
```

### 83815016f26105654bcf839a5052377ead900f26

[83815016f26105654bcf839a5052377ead900f26](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/83815016f26105654bcf839a5052377ead900f26)

Date: 2026-08-07T19:21:31-03:00 · Classification: Refactoring (inferred) · Systems: World & Visibility, Art & Animation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Refactor to RenderGraph TextureHandles
```

#### Original body

```text
Refactored the visibility mask pipeline to fully embrace URP 17 RenderGraph. VisibilityMaskRenderer now allocates an RTHandle. VisibilityPostProcessPass safely imports it via renderGraph.ImportTexture, binding it locally to the FogOfWar shader as _ProcessedMask. This decouples the shader from legacy SetGlobalTexture behavior and prepares the graph for intermediate Dilation and Blur passes.
```

#### Changed paths

```text
Project Grimhold/Assets/Art/Shaders/FogOfWar.shader
Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Visibility/VisibilityMaskRenderer.cs
Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/Visibility/VisibilityPostProcessPass.cs
```

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Restore spatial composition math to FogOfWar shader
```

#### Original body

```text
Reverted the FogOfWar shader from raw diagnostic mode back to its full spatial composition implementation. Validated the RenderGraph integration safely delivers _GlobalVisibilityMask and _GlobalVisibilityParams, allowing the shader to accurately map Screen UV to World Space to Mask UV using Unity 6's compute matrices.
```

#### Original subject

```text
implement dynamic render texture mask generation
```

#### Original body

```text
- Added VisibilityMaskRenderer to capture the geometric mesh into an off-screen RenderTexture.
- Added custom unlit shader for the visibility mesh to ensure pure solid color output.
- Injected global shader variables (_GlobalVisibilityMask and _GlobalVisibilityParams) to expose the mask to the rendering pipeline without logic coupling.
- Validated that logic remains CPU-bound and rendering GPU-bound.
```

```text
implement core geometric raycaster and mesh builder
```

#### Original body

```text
- Added VisibilitySettings for global data configuration.
- Added VisibilityObstacleCache to cache map geometry and support zero-allocation vertex proximity queries.
- Implemented VisibilityCalculator to perform angle-sorted vertex raycasting.
- Implemented VisibilityMeshBuilder to triangulate a debug mesh using Triangle Fan.
- All per-frame calculations are GC-free.
- Render pipeline integration deferred to Phase 2.
```

### 8ace4e2659578248bb5ce1aed0415452a84a4670

[8ace4e2659578248bb5ce1aed0415452a84a4670](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/8ace4e2659578248bb5ce1aed0415452a84a4670)

Date: 2026-08-07T23:01:33-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Networking & Sessions, UI

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Handle snapshot restore failures safely
```

#### Original body

```text
Improves host-migration snapshot recovery by making restoration validation explicit and fail-safe. `ValidateMatchController` now returns a detailed error instead of throwing, and restore failures trigger rollback, runner shutdown, and a return to the `MainMenu` scene. It also expands restored-object tracking with a global set so `IsRestoringObject` can recognize all restored objects (not just the actively processed one), improving authority/restore checks during migration.
```

#### Changed paths

```text
Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Networking/HostMigrationSnapshotRestorer.cs
Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Player/Presentation/RaidMenuPresenter.cs
```

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Track pending host-migration reconnects
```

#### Original body

```text
Add tracking for pending player reconnects during host-migration snapshot restores. HostMigrationSnapshotRestorer now records pending reconnect player objects and binds local camera/HUD when the restored object belongs to the local player. NetworkSpawnManager stores pending reconnects, admits players from that list on reconnection, and exposes removal when a pending character is defeated. Removed the previous reconnect-window timer logic. Minor changes: lifecycle runner flags set for new runner, and project settings updated to enable development/debugging and a URP asset reference added.
```

### 433db618caf8afeea7957a6ca8ec4c929a2eae17

[433db618caf8afeea7957a6ca8ec4c929a2eae17](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/433db618caf8afeea7957a6ca8ec4c929a2eae17)

Date: 2026-08-07T16:44:47-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Networking & Sessions

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Host migration: restore players and allow reconnections
```

#### Original body

```text
Pass restored player-object mapping from HostMigrationSnapshotRestorer to NetworkSpawnManager and use it to repopulate _admittedPlayers and _spawnedPlayers after a successful snapshot restore. Track host-migration completion time and open a reconnection window (up to 60s) so late joins during host-migration resume are accepted. Skip spawning for players already restored. Adjust admission logic ordering and improve logs. Also update ReportSnapshotRestoreResult signature to accept the optional restored mapping and propagate it to the restore flow.
```

```text

Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Networking/NetworkSpawnManager.cs
```

### 2c6431a323b3147c5996661dda58398d3ef14fd1

[2c6431a323b3147c5996661dda58398d3ef14fd1](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/2c6431a323b3147c5996661dda58398d3ef14fd1)

Date: 2026-08-07T00:01:05-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Movement, Inventory & Loot, Networking & Sessions, Documentation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Harden host migration snapshot restore flow
```

#### Original body

```text
Reworked host migration restore to be object-aware and fail-safe: dynamic/scene snapshot rebuild now validates IDs, runner/scene ownership, applies targeted EntityId fixups, enforces match controller integrity, and rolls back spawned objects on failure. Added `HostMigrationRestoreUtility.IsRestoreSpawn()` and replaced broad `!Object.IsResume` guards across gameplay/networked components so only objects actively being restored skip fresh `Spawned()` initialization. Also tightened migration lifecycle/startup checks (scene ref add validation, awaited runner shutdown), prevented duplicate snapshot-restore result reports, improved sanctuary presenter validity checks (`IsInSimulation`), and updated architecture docs to reflect HM-03 behavior and constraints.
```

### c08ca128c5d2af48dd9c6f8f9c9bc842e8317abf

[c08ca128c5d2af48dd9c6f8f9c9bc842e8317abf](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/c08ca128c5d2af48dd9c6f8f9c9bc842e8317abf)

Date: 2026-08-06T23:14:47-03:00 · Classification: Bug Fixes (inferred) · Systems: Extraction, Inventory & Loot

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
resolve multiplayer loadout synchronization using network RPC
```

#### Original body

```text
- Exposed LootCatalog getter in PlayerLootReceiver to retrieve the deterministic LootDefinitionCatalog.
- Refactored PlayerExtractionLootSaver to process extraction autoritatively on State Authority (Host) and dispatch updates to the owner peer (Input Authority) using RPC_SecureLootOnClient.
- Replaced string-based serialization with typed network arrays (int[] catalogIndices and int[] amounts) for efficient, robust transmission of extracted items.
- Guaranteed local peer isolation, where clients import their extracted loot directly into their respective in-memory LoadoutService upon receiving the RPC confirmation.
```

#### Changed paths

```text
Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/PlayerLootReceiver.cs
Project Grimhold/Assets/Scripts/Player/Extraction/PlayerExtractionLootSaver.cs
```

### ff47d8047bec8cbb9bbb85f6ed72e5a2797494ae

[ff47d8047bec8cbb9bbb85f6ed72e5a2797494ae](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/ff47d8047bec8cbb9bbb85f6ed72e5a2797494ae)

Date: 2026-08-06T22:40:25-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Inventory & Loot, Networking & Sessions, UI

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
implement pre-raid Loadout system and decoupled spawn injection
```

#### Original body

```text
- Created IPlayerLoadoutService to isolate the active pre-raid inventory from the persistent Stash.
- Added InMemoryPlayerLoadoutService, establishing the server as the authority for transfers and enforcing a 16-slot capacity limit.
- Created PlayerLoadoutInjector, attached to the player prefab, to fetch and inject the loadout asynchronously upon spawn. This fully decouples NetworkSpawnManager from inventory logic.
- Updated LobbyStashUI and LobbyStashPresenter to display both Stash and Loadout grids.
- Added support for transferring exact quantities (single vs full-stack) and implemented Take All / Leave All actions.
- Fixed an initialization timing issue in NetworkSpawnManager by assigning the ProfileIdString within the onBeforeSpawned callback, ensuring the loadout injector receives the correct profile ID when Spawned() is called.
```

```text
add lobby UI presenter and authoritative persistent profile tracking
```

### 83cae116832c81e272b158885243a5f070aad8bb

[83cae116832c81e272b158885243a5f070aad8bb](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/83cae116832c81e272b158885243a5f070aad8bb)

Date: 2026-08-06T21:43:35-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Movement, Inventory & Loot, Networking & Sessions

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Harden host migration snapshot restore flow
```

#### Original body

```text
Adds a dedicated `HostMigrationSnapshotRestorer` to own host-migration resume logic, restore dynamic/scene objects from snapshot state, and remap persisted `EntityId` references (projectiles, sanctuaries, extraction zones) to new network IDs. Runner composition now includes this restorer and wires `HostMigrationResume` to it. `NetworkSpawnManager` was extended with explicit host-migration restore states and coordination flags so scene-load readiness and snapshot-restore completion are synchronized before unblocking runtime rebind. Also updates multiple networked `Spawned()` initializers to skip default-state resets during resume (`!Object.IsResume`), preserving migrated state instead of overwriting it.
```

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add host migration runner lifecycle scaffolding
```

#### Original body

```text
Enables Fusion host migration auto-updates and introduces a new host migration lifecycle flow centered on runner replacement. `FusionSessionLauncher` now delegates runner composition to `NetworkRunnerFactory`, which builds and initializes runner-scoped services atomically with defensive data copies and cleanup on failure. Added `HostMigrationLifecycleController` to handle migration shutdown/scene transition/restart logic, plus `LauncherShutdownListener` to clear launcher references when a runner shuts down. Architecture docs were updated to describe the new HM-02 flow and current HM-03 limitation (snapshot restore still pending).
```

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add startup context for host migration resume
```

#### Original body

```text
Introduces immutable `SessionStartupContext` (`FreshSession` vs `HostMigrationResume`) and threads it through session startup to make bootstrap behavior explicit. Fresh-session-only steps are now gated across launcher, spawn manager, and match controller (host coordinator spawn, match phase init, and initial scene/entity bootstrap), while resume paths skip those operations and keep spawns blocked in a new `AwaitingHostMigrationRestore` state. Also adds edit mode tests for context validity/flags and updates architecture docs with the new lifecycle rules and current migration limitations.
```

### bc3a7f6f2202f6f73f3c70a3669ac21a50a02ff0

[bc3a7f6f2202f6f73f3c70a3669ac21a50a02ff0](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/bc3a7f6f2202f6f73f3c70a3669ac21a50a02ff0)

Date: 2026-08-03T23:27:28-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Inventory & Loot, Networking & Sessions, UI

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
implement authoritative inventory consumable system
```

#### Original body

```text
Implement an authoritative, data-driven consumable system for the raid inventory over Photon Fusion 2.1.

- Introduce `IConsumableEffect` and `HealthConsumableEffect` for static data-driven effects.
- Integrate `ConsumableDefinition` into `LootDefinition`.
- Add `IHealable` contract to `CharacterBase` to apply authoritative healing.
- Add `PlayerConsumableNetworkController` to process client intentions securely using RPC sequences and state authority validation.
- Add `LootConsumeContextActionProvider` to expose contextual actions in the UI and handle visual feedback upon server confirmation/rejection.
- Fix internal validation bugs with self-targeted `LootTransferRequest` extraction.

All changes adhere to the decoupled network architecture, restricting state mutation strictly to the State Authority.
```

### d2c4e3871809cf71728cf8678de04b7655e19a67

[d2c4e3871809cf71728cf8678de04b7655e19a67](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/d2c4e3871809cf71728cf8678de04b7655e19a67)

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add generated local raid minimap system
```

#### Original body

```text
Introduces a full local-only raid minimap pipeline: immutable `MinimapLayout` data, editor-time `MinimapLayoutGenerator` from graybox tilemaps, runtime projection math, and new `RaidMinimapGraphic`/`RaidMinimapView`/`RaidMinimapPresenter` components. Wires the minimap into `LocalPlayerHudBinder` and `NetworkPlayer.prefab` with masked UI, player marker, and sanctuary icon/edge-arrow behavior. Adds edit-mode tests for prefab/config integrity and projection rules, plus architecture docs updates to define TASK-68 boundaries and behavior.
```

### f1b40e9ba5f743cd62d19b4e84f93b45625145a8

[f1b40e9ba5f743cd62d19b4e84f93b45625145a8](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/f1b40e9ba5f743cd62d19b4e84f93b45625145a8)

Date: 2026-08-03T20:18:23-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add sanctuary ritual state and zone gating
```

#### Original body

```text
Implements TASK-55 by making `ExtractionSanctuary` an authoritative interactable ritual endpoint co-located with `ExtractionZone` under one identity. Adds replicated ritual lifecycle (`ExtractionRitualState` + timer), ritual progress snapshots, and config-driven ritual duration validation. Extraction now requires both zone availability and sanctuary ownership with completed ritual, while zone availability cannot be turned off after completion. Updates registry sanctuary unregistration semantics for shared-capability cleanup, adjusts prefab/scene composition to embed zone+collider+prompt on sanctuary objects, and expands edit/play mode tests plus architecture docs to cover ritual flow, authorization, and cleanup ordering.
```

### b01d86d9ebd53aa9f8fa6191dbe08454e25ce487

[b01d86d9ebd53aa9f8fa6191dbe08454e25ce487](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/b01d86d9ebd53aa9f8fa6191dbe08454e25ce487)

Date: 2026-08-03T01:35:49-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Inventory & Loot, UI, World & Visibility, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add extraction quota progress and loot provenance
```

#### Original body

```text
Implements US-13 individual extraction progress end-to-end: new progress contracts, receiver/defeat-source capabilities, registry support, and authoritative contribution flow from fatal damage, first container open, loot transfers, and pickups. Adds PlayerExtractionProgressController, ExtractionProgressDefeatSource, progress rules/snapshots, and wires prefab/config rewards and quota values.

Separates extraction countdown and quota models by renaming the old countdown snapshot to ExtractionCountdownSnapshot, updates controller/HUD usage, and adds broad EditMode/PlayMode coverage for rules, registry behavior, prefab composition, and simulation scenarios.

Also introduces loot first-acquisition provenance in transactions and containers, tracks eligible quantities to prevent duplicate credit, and decouples economy from progress by adding SellValuePerUnit and switching inventory/HUD value calculations to sell value.
```

### 341a5724d489b382099de44864d2b3a17935f7ab

[341a5724d489b382099de44864d2b3a17935f7ab](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/341a5724d489b382099de44864d2b3a17935f7ab)

Date: 2026-08-02T22:21:16-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, UI, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add extraction HUD and player visual states
```

#### Original body

```text
Implements TASK-29 presentation by wiring `PlayerExtractionController` into local HUD binding, extending `RaidHudPresenter/View` to render confirmed extraction countdown, cancellation, and completion states, and adding configurable cancellation feedback timing. Adds `PlayerExtractionPresenter` on `NetworkPlayer` to hide only body/combat visual roots when extracted while keeping gameplay/network systems active. Includes prefab wiring, new/updated EditMode tests, and architecture docs updates for extraction presentation boundaries.
```

### f8e765457c65839a34c96ef7f8921fbab0ed7165

[f8e765457c65839a34c96ef7f8921fbab0ed7165](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/f8e765457c65839a34c96ef7f8921fbab0ed7165)

Date: 2026-08-01T03:25:39-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Movement, Inventory & Loot, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Implement networked extraction flow
```

#### Original body

```text
Adds the extraction subsystem end-to-end: new ExtractionZone prefab and scene placement, extraction domain contracts/types, zone registry, and a PlayerExtractionController that runs authoritative start/cancel/complete logic with progress snapshots. Integrates extracted-state restrictions across player damage, interactions, loot transfer/drop, wires the registry in session startup, tightens enemy target validation/invalidation to avoid stale targets, and updates architecture docs with new EditMode coverage.
```

### 467c82c651c1cd28a363defad95d61f0e83b377b

[467c82c651c1cd28a363defad95d61f0e83b377b](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/467c82c651c1cd28a363defad95d61f0e83b377b)

Date: 2026-08-01T02:04:10-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Combat, Art & Animation, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Filter damage to explicit hitbox colliders
```

#### Original body

```text
Add explicit damage-collider registration in `EntityRegistry` (`TryRegisterDamageable`/`IsDamageCollider`) and wire `CharacterBase` plus player/enemy prefabs to register only configured `_damageHitboxes` for damage. Melee queries, projectiles, and spikes traps now ignore non-damage colliders while preserving collider-to-entity identity mapping, and slime attack clips were updated to call `OnAttackHit` with tests/docs expanded to cover the new behavior.
```

### 6e15b5fd384cd7b4097b75ddbdfedfc0b674151a

[6e15b5fd384cd7b4097b75ddbdfedfc0b674151a](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/6e15b5fd384cd7b4097b75ddbdfedfc0b674151a)

Date: 2026-08-01T01:44:47-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Combat, Art & Animation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Animator-driven enemy attacks + animation listener
```

#### Original body

```text
Adds slime attack AnimationClips and SlimeAttackConfig, updates the Slime AnimatorController with an Attack state and IsAttacking/OnAttack parameters, and updates the NetworkEnemyMelee prefab to include gizmo & animation-listener components. Introduces EnemyAttackAnimationListener (invoked by Animation Events) and refactors EnemyCombatAIController to commit attacks (TryCommitAttack), store a pending AttackRequest, and expose ExecutePendingDamage which applies damage only on State Authority when called from the animation hit frame. Exposes TriggerAttack on IAnimatorController/CharacterAnimatorView, updates EnemyAnimatorView to drive IsAttacking, and suppresses procedural swings in EnemyCombatPresenter. Adds gizmo drawing support for melee attacks.
```

### d2b486efdf16f8b3c169ea12ef93f26be300de06

[d2b486efdf16f8b3c169ea12ef93f26be300de06](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/d2b486efdf16f8b3c169ea12ef93f26be300de06)

Date: 2026-08-01T00:19:16-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Art & Animation, Documentation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Use animator and colliders for defeat
```

#### Original body

```text
Refactor defeat presentation to rely on the Animator and colliders instead of procedural rotation/alpha transitions.

- DefeatPresenterBase: removed per-sprite/transform transition logic and related serialized fields; added Collider2D[] _colliders and SetCollidersEnabled to disable/enable hit detection on death; simplified TriggerDefeat and CancelAndRestore to notify animator and toggle colliders.
- EnemyDefeatPresenter / PlayerDefeatPresenter: minor attribute and documentation updates; removed overridden hiding behavior.
- Prefabs (NetworkEnemy, NetworkPlayer, NetworkEnemyMelee): updated references to use collider components and adjusted collider size/offsets/local positions.

Result: visual defeat animation is handled by the Animator Controller; corpses no longer use scripted rotation/fade but colliders are disabled on defeat.
```


[8b76b481c8e7c42beb5349ebfc7e555f2eab24ff](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/8b76b481c8e7c42beb5349ebfc7e555f2eab24ff)

Flags: secondary

#### Original subject

```text
Normalize Slime sprite import & slicing settings
```

#### Original body

```text
Standardize import settings for placeholder Slime sprites. Changes include: set filterMode to Point, spriteMeshType to Full Rect, spritePixelsToUnits to 8, reduce platform maxTextureSize to 256, disable texture compression, fix sprite rects to 24x24 with centered pivots, and add SpriteEditor slicing metadata and spriteIDs. Applies to many .png.meta files under Assets/Placeholder/Slime to ensure consistent slicing, correct runtime scale, and smaller build textures.

Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>
```

### 2d90b14cfc7eb11c5819fe29bb1f02b93b28b245

[2d90b14cfc7eb11c5819fe29bb1f02b93b28b245](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/2d90b14cfc7eb11c5819fe29bb1f02b93b28b245)

Date: 2026-07-31T23:35:06-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Art & Animation, Documentation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add EnemyFSM Idle state & Defeated animation
```

#### Original body

```text
Introduce Idle as the default enemy state and add defeated animation/animator integration. Renamed Player.controller -> Character.controller and added Defeated state, OnDefeated parameter and transition; created Defeated.anim. Updated prefabs (NetworkEnemy, NetworkEnemyMelee, NetworkEnemyRanged) to include/slot an EnemyFSM component. Code changes: EnemyPatrolState renamed to EnemyIdleState and enum value Patrol -> Idle; EnemyFSM now starts in Idle, adds proxy sync (Render/SyncProxyCurrentState) and registers Idle state; EnemyCharacter now holds and uses EnemyFSM to transition to Dead on death and validates FSM dependency; CharacterAnimatorView triggers OnDefeated; EnemyCombatAIController disables attack by default for state authority. Added architecture doc EnemyFSMArchitecture.md. Coherent network/state sync and prefab wiring updates included.
```

```text
Implement authoritative inventory world drops
```

```text
- add a provider-driven context menu for personal inventory slots
- support single-unit and full-stack authoritative drop requests
- publish provisional loot pickups only after inventory extraction commits
- add deterministic drop placement with WorldCollision validation
- preserve existing container transfer interactions
- wire the drop controller and context menu into player prefabs
- add EditMode and PlayMode coverage for drop and menu behavior
- document the inventory world drop architecture
```

#### Original subject

```text
Add independent take-all loot transfers
```

#### Original body

```text
Add a local ordered take-all sequence that reuses the existing authoritative full-stack transfer pipeline.

Process visible container stacks independently, continue after individual failures, preserve successful transfers, refresh both panels between requests, and cancel unsent operations when the loot screen closes.

Add the serialized Take All button, contextual interaction states, architecture documentation, and coverage for ordering, inventory capacity, rejection continuation, and cancellation.
```

### 9ba5649d11f8ace8bbe36fe7ca873d26d810c316

[9ba5649d11f8ace8bbe36fe7ca873d26d810c316](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/9ba5649d11f8ace8bbe36fe7ca873d26d810c316)

Date: 2026-07-28T19:37:56-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Networking & Sessions

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Implement networked trap state machine and DartTrap
```

#### Original body

```text
Refactor traps to run as authoritative networked entities and add a networked DartTrap workflow.

- BaseTrap converted to NetworkBehaviour with a TickTimer-driven state machine (Ready, Telegraphing, Active, InCooldown). Replaces coroutine timing with FixedUpdateNetwork and Render for visuals. Adds lifecycle hooks (OnEnterTelegraphing/Active/Cooldown/Ready) for subclasses.
- DartTrap rewritten to use RangedAttackConfig + FusionProjectileSpawner to spawn networked projectiles. Adds networked counters/timers for dart bursts and auto-finds the spawner if unassigned.
- Added a new ScriptableObject asset (DartTrapConfig) for ranged attack settings and updated prefabs to reference new components.
- FusionProjectileSpawner now attempts to resolve an IDamageResolver automatically if none assigned.
- TrapInfo simplified to a pure configuration ScriptableObject (defaults added); removed its runtime/networked state.
- SpikesTrap marked DisallowMultipleComponent; prefabs updated accordingly.

These changes make trap behavior deterministic and authoritative on the host and reuse the existing projectile/damage systems for environmental hazards.

Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>
```

### d40315c294deee4b7cbffcff8e2e831ac047927b

[d40315c294deee4b7cbffcff8e2e831ac047927b](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/d40315c294deee4b7cbffcff8e2e831ac047927b)

Date: 2026-07-28T19:39:26-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Inventory & Loot, UI, Input, Build & Tooling, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
implement raid pause and defeat menu overlay
```

#### Original body

```text
Implement in-raid menu overlay providing pause functionality and automated defeat state presentation.
- Add RaidMenuView and RaidMenuPresenter components to manage in-raid menu UI layout, basic controls display, resume/abandon actions, and local gameplay input suppression.
- Support player defeat state by automatically presenting the defeat menu, locking character actions, enabling mouse cursor, and disabling resume option while keeping abandon option available.
- Update PlayerInputReader and RaidInventoryPresenter to prioritize active inventory screen closure when pressing Escape, preventing accidental menu toggle.
- Add native serialized EventSystem with InputSystemUIInputModule under LocalGameplayHud in NetworkPlayer.prefab to ensure UI pointer interaction across all gameplay scenes.
- Handle raid abandonment with controlled NetworkRunner shutdown and scene transition to MainMenu.
- Add EditMode and PlayMode unit/integration test suites covering Escape key input priority, defeat auto-open behavior, view rendering, and HUD bindings.
```

#### Original subject

```text
unify facing and attacks with simulated aim
```

#### Original body

```text
Resolve player aim from AimWorldPosition after tick movement and persist it in FacingDirection.

Make melee and ranged attacks consume the same validated facing direction, preserve it for missing/default/invalid input, and enforce movement-before-combat execution order.

Add the Characters sorting layer for player and enemy visuals so weapon attacks remain visible in every direction while preserving front/back weapon ordering.
```

### 93508de8c9f4e22b395b70b4aee0f41b3de0c772

#### Original subject

```text
Add synchronized dynamic breakable loot drops
```

#### Original body

```text
Implemented dynamic networked breakable objects with authoritative damage handling and deterministic random loot drops.
Added BreakableObject implementing IDamageable.
Added State Authority destruction and duplicate-drop protection.
Added dynamic Breakables spawn group support.
Added synchronized NetworkLootPickup initialization using catalog indices and quantities.
Added world loot sprite sorting layer and sorting order configuration.
Added Escape support to close the raid inventory.
Added prefab, architecture documentation, and automated tests.
Preserved pickup interaction, inventory transfer, and synchronized despawn flow.
```

### a477674bb24dbbf35c942c6ccad8cc1647a7cd6b

[a477674bb24dbbf35c942c6ccad8cc1647a7cd6b](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/a477674bb24dbbf35c942c6ccad8cc1647a7cd6b)

Date: 2026-07-22T23:33:24-03:00 · Classification: Other Changes (unknown) · Systems: Combat, Inventory & Loot, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
make defeated enemies persist as lootable containers
```

#### Original body

```text
Reused the enemy’s existing NetworkObject as its corpse instead of spawning a separate corpse prefab.
Added the shared loot container and interaction components to enemy prefabs.
Kept enemy loot unavailable while alive and enabled it authoritatively after fatal damage.
Disabled enemy movement and combat after death while preserving the body for future death animations.
Added an interaction-layer trigger for corpse inspection.
Updated entity registration to support damage, interaction, and loot capabilities under the same entity ID.
Removed the obsolete LootCorpse prefab.
Updated architecture documentation and automated tests.
Verified all tests: 229 EditMode and 24 PlayMode tests passed.
```

### e320fdc0fd7ec380a2c298e3d384b746caf91250

[e320fdc0fd7ec380a2c298e3d384b746caf91250](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/e320fdc0fd7ec380a2c298e3d384b746caf91250)

Date: 2026-07-21T21:48:48-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Extraction, Inventory & Loot, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
add authoritative reusable network loot containers
```

#### Original body

```text
- Add NetworkLootContainer with replicated catalog-indexed contents,
  initialization state, runtime availability, and change sequencing.
- Validate initial content atomically and register loot-source capabilities and
  colliders through the runner-scoped EntityRegistry.
- Preserve initialized contents and disable transfers when grouped registration
  fails.
- Add authoritative runtime availability changes without modifying contents,
  registration, or LootChangeSequence.
- Add LootTransferTransaction with strict extraction validation, receive
  validation, extraction commit, and receive commit ordering.
- Enforce validate/commit contracts in player and container endpoints, treating
  impossible commit-time states as integration errors.
- Separate pickup-specific presentation feedback from generic loot reception so
  container transfers do not display pickup toasts.
- Add PlayerLootTransferNetworkController with primitive-only Fusion RPC
  payloads, authoritative range and quantity resolution, and local
  confirmation reconstruction.
- Support one legitimate client request in flight, a non-overwritable
  authoritative pending request, and bounded last-request idempotency.
- Handle pending duplicates, processed duplicates, payload conflicts, busy
  sequences, stale sequences, malformed confirmations, and unresolved loot
  metadata for rejected transfers.
- Keep request, pending, cache, and confirmation state local and non-networked.
- Add LootContainer and separate development harness prefabs without adding
  debug tooling to production player prefabs or scenes.
- Add Editor/Development-only controls for public requests, duplicate and
  conflict envelopes, busy sequences, and authoritative availability changes.
- Update base, melee, and ranged player prefabs to include the transfer network
  behaviour required by Fusion.
- Add EditMode coverage for initialization, grouped registration, transactions,
  request lifecycle, idempotency, confirmation reconstruction, and prefab
  composition.
- Update loot architecture and base-contract documentation to reflect the final
  implementation.
```

```text
implement temporary player loot inventory
```

#### Original body

```text
Added configurable gameplay slot capacity to PlayerLootReceiver with stacking support and InventoryFull rejection for new loot IDs.

Implemented authoritative loot extraction with Validate/Commit separation, partial extraction, empty-stack removal, and LootChangeSequence updates.

Preserved the replicated NetworkDictionary as the single source of truth, the existing pickup transaction, and snapshot-based presentation.

Added pure inventory rule tests, configured the base player prefab with 16 slots, and updated the loot architecture and contract documentation.
```

```text
unify loot inventory and transfer contracts
```

```text
Added unified loot transfer request, result, and typed failure reasons.
Added segregated content, quantity, slot-capacity, reception, and extraction contracts.
Evolved LootEntry into the canonical stack value object.
Migrated PlayerLootReceiver to explicit prevalidation and commit phases.
Preserved the authoritative pickup reservation, rollback, and consumption flow.
Removed the legacy grant/receive contracts.
Added comprehensive EditMode contract and migration tests.
Updated loot and player-interaction architecture documentation in English.
Preserved HUD, presentation, RPCs, networked properties, prefabs, and scenes.
```

### 075df797c2c54c7f69b0d330c29a4cd3a43e94f8

[075df797c2c54c7f69b0d330c29a4cd3a43e94f8](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/075df797c2c54c7f69b0d330c29a4cd3a43e94f8)

Date: 2026-07-21T16:46:07-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Inventory & Loot, UI, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
implement networked loot and local interaction HUD
```

#### Original body

```text
- Implement authoritative loot collection using a NetworkDictionary with deterministic indices.
- Add safe read-only APIs for querying loot contents, quantities, and derived total value.
- Add change sequences and targeted RPC notifications for the owning player.
- Separate interaction target selection from authoritative execution.
- Add local predictive target detection for displaying the interaction prompt without executing gameplay.
- Record typed results for accepted and rejected interaction attempts.
- Publish interaction results from Render to keep presentation outside network simulation.
- Add a local HUD with an interaction prompt, result feedback, loot list, accumulated value, and pickup toast.
- Enable the HUD exclusively for the player with Input Authority.
- Prevent duplicate feedback using authoritative sequences and local deduplication.
- Suppress generic success feedback when the interaction consumes a loot pickup.
- Integrate the HUD and its dependencies into NetworkPlayer.prefab.
- Update the melee and ranged variants with the inherited NetworkBehaviours.
- Expand tests for interaction resolution, loot catalog contracts, presentation payloads, and HUD lifecycle.
- Update the interaction and loot architecture documentation.

Validation:
- Runtime and Editor assemblies compile without errors.
- Fusion IL weaving completes successfully.
- The base player prefab and both variants import successfully.
- The Host/Client flow was manually validated using two game instances.
- Local HUD isolation, loot replication, targeted RPCs, and remote pickup despawning were verified.
```

### 9cd8c8b497ae4fa1b1389c1296e16ed0fc50b379

[9cd8c8b497ae4fa1b1389c1296e16ed0fc50b379](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/9cd8c8b497ae4fa1b1389c1296e16ed0fc50b379)

Date: 2026-07-20T14:33:59-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Networking & Sessions, UI, Persistence & Backend, Build & Tooling, Documentation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
add late-join prevention and scene spawn-point policy architecture
```

#### Original body

```text
- Enforced authoritative coordinator persistence by spawning the NetworkMatchController with Photon Fusion's managed `NetworkSpawnFlags.DontDestroyOnLoad` flags.
- Replaced global ActiveScene fallbacks with runner-scoped scene resolution using `runner.SceneManager.MainRunnerScene` exclusively.
- Implemented a declarative scene configuration system (`SceneSpawnPointPolicy`) to explicitly declare whether a scene requires spawn points (`Required`) or not (`NotRequired`).
- Decoupled spatial scene configuration into `NetworkSpawnSceneConfiguration` and spawn group definitions (`SpawnGroupType`, `SpawnGroupDefinition`).
- Enforced a fail-closed spawning pipeline using incremental load generations and tracking states (Pending, Processing, Completed, Failed) to deduplicate callback execution.
- Added strict validations: `CanUseCurrentSceneSpawnPoints` for spatial-based entities and `CanSpawnAtExplicitTransform` for explicit coordinate-based spawns.
- Fixed an enemy spawning bug by refactoring `_spawnedEnemies` from a dictionary to a list to prevent duplicate key exceptions.
- Added visual/console feedback message when a client fails or is rejected to join an already started session.
- Updated launcher start/shutdown lifecycles with secure try-finally blocks and async cleanup.
- Refactored all networking code and architecture documents to align with the new spawn-point terminology.
```

### b890317588aa2530670097f84d243941b4888e2e

[b890317588aa2530670097f84d243941b4888e2e](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/b890317588aa2530670097f84d243941b4888e2e)

Date: 2026-07-21T20:24:19-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Movement, Networking & Sessions, Art & Animation, Documentation

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add enemy AI, FSM, and shared presentation base
```

#### Original body

```text
Introduce enemy systems and refactor shared presentation/API contracts. Adds EnemyFSM, IEnemyState and concrete states (Patrol/Chase/Attack/Dead), EnemyMovementAIController and EnemyCombatAIController, plus NetworkEnemyMelee/NetworkEnemyRanged prefabs. Add shared presentation components (CharacterAnimatorView, CombatPresenterBase, DefeatPresenterBase) and interfaces (IAnimatorController, ICombatController, IMovementState) and adapt player presenters to inherit them. Make combat/attack/projectile systems entity-agnostic (DamageResolver, MeleeAttack, RangedAttack, FusionProjectileSpawner), update NetworkSpawnManager to support spawn-group prefab lists, and include architecture doc EnemyCombatArchitecture.md.
```

### 745a21750d7c26fb2eacd81a5fa3e8e817e87cb8

[745a21750d7c26fb2eacd81a5fa3e8e817e87cb8](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/745a21750d7c26fb2eacd81a5fa3e8e817e87cb8)

Date: 2026-07-18T00:22:46-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Inventory & Loot, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
implement synchronized loot pickup system
```

#### Original body

```text
add LootId and typed loot delivery contracts
add LootDefinition support for stable loot identifiers
replace string-based interaction results with typed failure reasons
implement NetworkLootPickup with authoritative reservation, delivery, and despawn
implement PlayerLootReceiver with Fusion lifecycle registration
extend EntityRegistry with loot receiver registration and lookup
preserve multiple capabilities under the same EntityId
prevent duplicate rewards from concurrent interaction requests
restore pickup availability when loot delivery is rejected
add EditMode coverage for loot contracts, receiver state, registry behavior, and interaction results
add loot interaction architecture documentation
add reusable loot pickup prefab and graybox-ready configuration
```

### 97e33636ffef67e0fa02514b757127bdbce9d15a

[97e33636ffef67e0fa02514b757127bdbce9d15a](https://github.com/Astro-Rift-Games/ProjectGrimhold/commit/97e33636ffef67e0fa02514b757127bdbce9d15a)

Date: 2026-07-17T23:29:00-03:00 · Classification: Features (inferred) · Systems: Combat, Abilities, Inventory & Loot, Build & Tooling, Documentation, Tests

Flags: primary or unclassified

#### Original subject

```text
Add authoritative loot pickup interaction flow
```

#### Original body

```text
Introduces a new loot interaction contract and transaction pipeline: `LootId`, `LootGrantRequest`, `LootReceiveResult`, `ILootReceiver`, and `IPickup`. `EntityRegistry` now tracks loot receivers, and `InteractionResult` was refactored to use typed failure reasons with success/reject factories.

Adds `NetworkLootPickup` to enforce state-authority pickup handling, grant loot atomically, and despawn only on successful grants, plus `PlayerLootReceiver` as an authoritative per-player loot container. Updates `LootDefinition` to expose `LootId`, expands/edit-fixes related interaction tests, adds dedicated loot tests, and documents the architecture in `Docs/Architecture/LootInteractionArchitecture.md`.
```
